Papercusp portfolio audit · WI-42352

August 2026 unshipped plans with no active agent

A complete, identity-exact audit of lifecycle, progress, blockers, overlap, decision evidence, and recommended disposition for every August plan that was unshipped and had no live agent or claim at the audit cut.

Audit cut: 26 August 2026, America/New_York · Workspace: papercusp-workspace · 369 unshipped August identities examined · 18 live identities excluded · 351 inactive identities classified

Bottom line: this is primarily lifecycle debt, not a 351-plan implementation queue. 196 should stay superseded or be retired, 60 should be reconciled and closed, 91 merit scoped resumption/reconciliation, and only 4 look genuinely unstarted.

Recommended handling

  • Close: verify acceptance/code truth, reconcile stale lifecycle or item residue, and close without reimplementation.
  • Resume: re-baseline blockers and current code first; execute only surviving gaps. Related-plan references trigger scope reconciliation, not an automatic merge.
  • Start: revalidate value and scope before scheduling the small genuinely unstarted set.
  • Supersede: retain explicit supersession history; archive empty, fixture, or obsolete plans instead of treating them as product backlog.

Method and evidence quality

  1. Started from 369 August-created, unshipped harness+slug identities.
  2. Ran a canonical per-plan active-only assignment check; excluded 18 identities with live agents or claims.
  3. Fetched parsed plan structures for all 351 remaining identities: frontmatter, Now state/next, item statuses and blockers, decisions, linked features, and integrity diagnostics.
  4. Detected 109 plans whose multi-plan response had shortened long decision bodies. Re-fetched all 1,016 affected decisions in harness-scoped batches, followed exact scratch references where the aggregate budget returned them out of band, and verified zero missing, extra, duplicate, null, or still-truncated decision records.
  5. Applied one strongest disposition per identity in this order: explicit supersession; no executable items; terminal/completion evidence; fixture/probe; human residue; current blocker; partial completion; effectively unstarted. Cross-plan mentions were reviewed conservatively and never treated as automatic merger authority.

This report recommends portfolio treatment; it does not mutate plan lifecycle or claim work. “No active agent” is the live audit-cut fact, not a promise that ownership cannot change later.
